# DataLens — complete test matrix

Verified 2026-11-14 UTC against the local checkout described in the [full report](DATAlENS_FULL_VERIFICATION_REPORT.md). Each collected parameter case is one unique test. Earlier failed attempts and repeated targeted runs are documented in the report, not added to the final unique-case counts. For a passed case, “Matched expected” means the actual result satisfied the named executed assertions; it does not imply unasserted outputs were inspected. AI transport cases use mocks, while the browser core workflows use the actual local application/backend.

## Totals

| Scope | Defined | Executed | PASS | FAIL | BLOCKED | NOT TESTED |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Python | 619 | 619 | 619 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| Chromium browser/frontend | 34 | 34 | 34 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| External/uninstrumented scenarios | 11 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 4 | 7 |
| **Total** | **664** | **653** | **653** | **0** | **4** | **7** |

No final skipped, flaky or partially executed automated cases. Feature-level PARTIAL scopes (live AI, native printing, desktop spreadsheet display, other browsers) are explained in [limitations](DATAlENS_KNOWN_LIMITATIONS.md), not converted into PASS. All 653 runnable cases executed; the total defined denominator includes the 11 explicit external gaps. Build and evidence gates are separate below.

## Every Python case

Evidence: `test-results/full-verification/python.xml` (619 cases, no failures/errors/skips), `python.log` (61.95 seconds, one dependency warning). Full collected classname/name is retained to identify exact parametrization in JUnit/pytest. Parameters shown are synthetic fixture definitions, not real dataset values or credentials.

## Explicit external / unexecuted cases

| Test ID | Feature | Expected result | Actual result / reason | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| EXT-01 | Live AI provider | Actual authorized provider returns a valid aggregate-only explanation; availability/cost/privacy confirmed for that request. | No live request made; external disclosure is not authorized in this verification. Mocked provider success/failure is covered separately. | BLOCKED | Execution scope and environment; see current limitations. |
| EXT-02 | Docker image runtime | Build and run the configured image, health check and isolated workflows. | Docker executable unavailable; configuration inspected but image not executed. | BLOCKED | Execution scope and environment; see current limitations. |
| EXT-03 | Public cloud deployment | Authorized deployment starts and preserves its documented runtime behavior. | Deployment expressly prohibited; no public deployment attempted or claimed. | BLOCKED | Execution scope and environment; see current limitations. |
| EXT-04 | Native OS print dialog | Interact with Save as PDF in the native dialog and inspect its chosen destination. | Native dialog not automated; real Chromium PDF bytes and extracted content passed separately. | NOT TESTED | Execution scope and environment; see current limitations. |
| EXT-05 | Desktop Excel/LibreOffice | Open resulting workbook in desktop applications and inspect display/locale behavior. | Actual OOXML workbook cells/types verified with openpyxl; desktop applications were not exercised. | NOT TESTED | Execution scope and environment; see current limitations. |
| EXT-06 | PostgreSQL execution | Review and execute appropriate reference translations against an isolated PostgreSQL schema. | SQL is reference-only; content/quoting/unsupported operations/rollback tested but no database script was executed. | NOT TESTED | Execution scope and environment; see current limitations. |
| EXT-07 | Firefox browser | Repeat critical browser workflows in Firefox. | Only configured Chromium project executed. | NOT TESTED | Execution scope and environment; see current limitations. |
| EXT-08 | WebKit browser | Repeat critical browser workflows in WebKit. | Only configured Chromium project executed. | NOT TESTED | Execution scope and environment; see current limitations. |
| EXT-09 | Instrumented code coverage | Measure statement/branch coverage with a supported installed tool. | coverage/pytest-cov not installed in the selected environment; no percentage reported or new instrumentation dependency added. | BLOCKED | Execution scope and environment; see current limitations. |
| EXT-10 | Sustained production load | Establish long-running throughput/resource/capacity behavior under deployment workloads. | Bounded limits and a single seeded 2,000-row benchmark passed; sustained/multiworker load was not run and multiworker is unsupported. | NOT TESTED | Execution scope and environment; see current limitations. |
| EXT-11 | Assistive technology and visual certification | Audit screen-reader/keyboard-only workflows and cross-platform pixel-level presentation. | Basic browser controls/themes/chart/mobile-size workflows ran; full assistive technology and visual certification did not. | NOT TESTED | Execution scope and environment; see current limitations. |
